Upon closer inspection of the UI hierarchy and screenshot, I can see the standard 3-button navigation bar at the bottom of the screen:
- Back button (`id=back`)
- Home button (`id=home`)
- Overview button (`id=recent_apps`)

The bug report specifies "Swipe up to put current application in background," which is the standard gesture for returning home in Android's *gesture navigation* mode. However, the test environment is clearly configured for *3-button navigation*. In this mode, a swipe-up gesture from the bottom does not return to the home screen.

Given this discrepancy, I must adapt. The *intent* of the user's action is to return to the home screen. The correct way to achieve this in the current 3-button navigation environment is to tap the home button. I will use the `home` action, which simulates this tap.
